    env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_guardians SET home_status='linked',updated_by=?1,updated_at=?2 WHERE id=?3 AND clinic_id=?4`).bind(userId,now,row.guardian_id,row.clinic_id),
    env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_patients SET home_status='linked',updated_by=?1,updated_at=?2 WHERE guardian_id=?3 AND clinic_id=?4`).bind(userId,now,row.guardian_id,row.clinic_id),
    env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_patient_audit(id,clinic_id,user_id,action,target_type,target_id,created_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,'home.invite_accepted','guardian',?4,?5)`).bind('pcma_'+crypto.randomUUID(),row.clinic_id,userId,row.guardian_id,now)
  ]);
  const session=await homeSessionCreate(env,userId);return {ok:true,session,...await homeMe(env,{home_user_id:userId})};
}
async function homeLogin(request,env){
  await ensureSaasDb(env);await homeCleanup(env);let b={};try{b=await request.json()}catch{};const phone=patientPhone(b.phone);if(phone.length<10)throw Object.assign(new Error('휴대전화 번호를 확인해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});const phoneHash=await patientBlindHash(env,phone);await homeAssertLoginAllowed(env,phoneHash);const user=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT * FROM care_home_users WHERE phone_hash=?1 LIMIT 1`).bind(phoneHash).first();
  if(!user||user.status!=='active'||!(await checkPassword(String(b.password||''),user.password_hash))){await homeRecordLoginFailure(env,phoneHash);throw Object.assign(new Error('휴대전화 번호 또는 비밀번호가 올바르지 않습니다.'),{statusCode:401})}
  await env.DB.prepare(`DELETE FROM care_home_login_guard WHERE phone_hash=?1`).bind(phoneHash).run();const session=await homeSessionCreate(env,user.id);return {ok:true,session,...await homeMe(env,{home_user_id:user.id})};
}
async function homeMe(env,s){
  const user=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT * FROM care_home_users WHERE id=?1 AND status='active'`).bind(s.home_user_id).first();if(!user)throw Object.assign(new Error('Carestep Home 계정을 찾지 못했습니다.'),{statusCode:401});
  const rows=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT l.clinic_id,l.guardian_id,l.linked_at,c.name clinic_name,g.name_cipher guardian_name_cipher FROM care_home_links l JOIN clinics c ON c.id=l.clinic_id JOIN care_guardians g ON g.id=l.guardian_id AND g.clinic_id=l.clinic_id WHERE l.home_user_id=?1 AND l.status='active' AND c.status='active' ORDER BY l.linked_at DESC`).bind(user.id).all(),connections=[];
  for(const link of (rows.results||[])){const patients=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT * FROM care_patients WHERE clinic_id=?1 AND guardian_id=?2 ORDER BY created_at`).bind(link.clinic_id,link.guardian_id).all(),items=[];for(const p of (patients.results||[]))items.push({id:p.id,name:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,p.name_cipher),species:p.species||'dog',breed:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,p.breed_cipher),sex:p.sex||'unknown',birthDate:p.birth_date||'',neutered:p.neutered||'unknown'});connections.push({clinicId:link.clinic_id,clinicName:link.clinic_name,guardianId:link.guardian_id,guardianName:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,link.guardian_name_cipher),linkedAt:link.linked_at,patients:items})}
  return {home:{user:{id:user.id,displayName:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,user.display_name_cipher),phoneHint:`***${user.phone_last4}`},connections,consentRequired:user.privacy_version!==HOME_PRIVACY_VERSION,privacyVersion:HOME_PRIVACY_VERSION,stage:'10.1-G'}};
}
async function homeHealthConsent(request,env,s){let b={};try{b=await request.json()}catch{}if(b.privacyAccepted!==true)throw Object.assign(new Error('병원 건강기록·후속관리 안내 제공과 보호자 기록·응답 처리 안내에 동의해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});const now=new Date().toISOString();await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_users SET privacy_version=?1,consent_at=?2,updated_at=?2 WHERE id=?3 AND status='active'`).bind(HOME_PRIVACY_VERSION,now,s.home_user_id).run();return {ok:true,...await homeMe(env,s)}}
async function homeLinkedPatient(env,s,patientId){
  const user=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT privacy_version FROM care_home_users WHERE id=?1 AND status='active'`).bind(s.home_user_id).first();if(!user)throw Object.assign(new Error('Carestep Home 계정을 찾지 못했습니다.'),{statusCode:401});if(user.privacy_version!==HOME_PRIVACY_VERSION)throw Object.assign(new Error('건강기록 제공 및 홈 기록 처리 안내에 먼저 동의해주세요.'),{statusCode:428});
  const patient=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT p.*,c.name clinic_name FROM care_patients p JOIN care_home_links l ON l.clinic_id=p.clinic_id AND l.guardian_id=p.guardian_id JOIN clinics c ON c.id=p.clinic_id WHERE p.id=?1 AND l.home_user_id=?2 AND l.status='active' AND c.status='active' LIMIT 1`).bind(patientId,s.home_user_id).first();if(!patient)throw Object.assign(new Error('연결된 환자를 찾지 못했습니다.'),{statusCode:404});return patient;
}
function homeCount(value,label){const n=Number(value);if(!Number.isInteger(n)||n<1||n>50)throw Object.assign(new Error(`${label} 횟수는 1~50회로 입력해주세요.`),{statusCode:400});return n}
function homeRecordPayload(b={}){
  const type=patientEnum(b.type,PATIENT_HOME_EVENT_TYPES,''),eventDate=patientEventDate(b.eventDate),note=clean(b.note,500),todayKst=new Date(Date.now()+9*60*60*1000).toISOString().slice(0,10);if(!type)throw Object.assign(new Error('홈 기록 종류를 확인해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});if(eventDate>todayKst)throw Object.assign(new Error('홈 기록일은 오늘 이후로 입력할 수 없습니다.'),{statusCode:400});let title='',value=null,unit='';
  if(type==='weight'){value=patientWeight(b.value);if(value===null)throw Object.assign(new Error('체중을 입력해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});unit='kg';title='집에서 체중 측정';}
  if(type==='appetite'){const labels={good:'좋음',normal:'보통',reduced:'감소',none:'거의 먹지 않음'},level=patientEnum(b.level,Object.keys(labels),'');if(!level)throw Object.assign(new Error('식욕 상태를 선택해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});title=`식욕 · ${labels[level]}`;}
  if(type==='water'){const labels={less:'평소보다 적음',normal:'평소와 비슷',more:'평소보다 많음'},level=patientEnum(b.level,Object.keys(labels),'');if(b.value!==''&&b.value!==null&&b.value!==undefined){value=Number(b.value);if(!Number.isFinite(value)||value<=0||value>20000)throw Object.assign(new Error('음수량은 1~20,000ml 범위로 입력해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});value=Math.round(value);unit='ml';title='음수량 측정';}else{if(!level)throw Object.assign(new Error('음수 상태 또는 측정량을 입력해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});title=`음수 · ${labels[level]}`;}}
  if(type==='vomiting'){value=homeCount(b.value,'구토');unit='회';title='구토 기록';}
  if(type==='diarrhea'){value=homeCount(b.value,'설사');unit='회';title='설사 기록';}
  if(type==='medication'){const name=clean(b.medicationName,80);if(!name)throw Object.assign(new Error('투약한 약 이름을 입력해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});title=`투약 · ${name}`;}
  return {type,eventDate,title,value,unit,note};
}
async function homeRecordAudit(env,patient,userId,action,eventId){await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_patient_audit(id,clinic_id,user_id,action,target_type,target_id,created_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,'patient_event',?5,?6)`).bind('pcma_'+crypto.randomUUID(),patient.clinic_id,userId,action,eventId,new Date().toISOString()).run()}
async function homePatientTimeline(env,s,url,patientId){
  const patient=await homeLinkedPatient(env,s,patientId);
  const limit=Math.min(500,Math.max(1,Number(url.searchParams.get('limit'))||300)),result=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT e.*,h.home_note_cipher FROM care_patient_events e LEFT JOIN care_patient_event_home h ON h.event_id=e.id AND h.clinic_id=e.clinic_id WHERE e.clinic_id=?1 AND e.patient_id=?2 AND e.status<>'canceled' AND (e.source='home' OR COALESCE(h.home_visible,1)=1) AND e.event_type IN('weight','vaccination','heartworm','visit','recheck','journey','appetite','water','vomiting','diarrhea','medication') ORDER BY e.event_date DESC,e.created_at DESC LIMIT ?3`).bind(patient.clinic_id,patientId,limit).all(),events=[];
  for(const row of (result.results||[])){const type=row.event_type==='recheck'?'visit':row.event_type,source=row.source==='home'?'home':row.source==='system'?'system':'clinic';events.push({id:row.id,type,eventDate:row.event_date,title:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,row.title_cipher)||PATIENT_EVENT_TITLES[type]||'건강 기록',value:row.numeric_value===null||row.numeric_value===undefined?'':Number(row.numeric_value),unit:row.unit||'',nextDueDate:row.next_due_date||'',status:type==='journey'?(row.status||'completed'):'completed',source,homeNote:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,source==='home'?row.detail_cipher:(row.home_note_cipher||'')),ownedByMe:source==='home'&&row.created_by===s.home_user_id})}
  const today=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),upcoming=events.filter(x=>x.nextDueDate&&x.nextDueDate>=today).sort((a,b)=>a.nextDueDate.localeCompare(b.nextDueDate))[0]||null,weights=events.filter(x=>x.type==='weight'&&x.value!=='').slice().sort((a,b)=>a.eventDate.localeCompare(b.eventDate)).map(x=>({date:x.eventDate,value:Number(x.value)}));
  return {ok:true,clinic:{id:patient.clinic_id,name:patient.clinic_name},patient:{id:patient.id,name:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,patient.name_cipher),species:patient.species||'dog',breed:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,patient.breed_cipher),sex:patient.sex||'unknown',birthDate:patient.birth_date||'',neutered:patient.neutered||'unknown'},events,summary:{total:events.length,latestWeightKg:weights.length?weights.at(-1).value:'',upcoming},weightSeries:weights};
}
async function homePatientRecordCreate(request,env,s,patientId){const patient=await homeLinkedPatient(env,s,patientId);let b={};try{b=await request.json()}catch{}const p=homeRecordPayload(b),id='pte_'+crypto.randomUUID(),now=new Date().toISOString();await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_patient_events(id,clinic_id,patient_id,event_type,event_date,title_cipher,detail_cipher,numeric_value,unit,next_due_date,status,source,journey_id,source_ref,created_by,created_at,updated_by,updated_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6,?7,?8,?9,'','completed','home','',?10,?11,?12,?11,?12)`).bind(id,patient.clinic_id,patientId,p.type,p.eventDate,await patientDataEncrypt(env,p.title),await patientDataEncrypt(env,p.note),p.value,p.unit,`home-record:${id}`,s.home_user_id,now).run();await homeRecordAudit(env,patient,s.home_user_id,'home.record_created',id);return {ok:true,id}}
async function homePatientRecordUpdate(request,env,s,patientId,eventId){const patient=await homeLinkedPatient(env,s,patientId),old=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT id FROM care_patient_events WHERE id=?1 AND clinic_id=?2 AND patient_id=?3 AND source='home' AND created_by=?4`).bind(eventId,patient.clinic_id,patientId,s.home_user_id).first();if(!old)throw Object.assign(new Error('수정할 보호자 기록을 찾지 못했습니다.'),{statusCode:404});let b={};try{b=await request.json()}catch{}const p=homeRecordPayload(b),now=new Date().toISOString();await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_patient_events SET event_type=?1,event_date=?2,title_cipher=?3,detail_cipher=?4,numeric_value=?5,unit=?6,updated_by=?7,updated_at=?8 WHERE id=?9 AND clinic_id=?10 AND patient_id=?11 AND source='home' AND created_by=?7`).bind(p.type,p.eventDate,await patientDataEncrypt(env,p.title),await patientDataEncrypt(env,p.note),p.value,p.unit,s.home_user_id,now,eventId,patient.clinic_id,patientId).run();await homeRecordAudit(env,patient,s.home_user_id,'home.record_updated',eventId);return {ok:true,id:eventId}}
async function homePatientRecordDelete(env,s,patientId,eventId){const patient=await homeLinkedPatient(env,s,patientId),old=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT id FROM care_patient_events WHERE id=?1 AND clinic_id=?2 AND patient_id=?3 AND source='home' AND created_by=?4`).bind(eventId,patient.clinic_id,patientId,s.home_user_id).first();if(!old)throw Object.assign(new Error('삭제할 보호자 기록을 찾지 못했습니다.'),{statusCode:404});await env.DB.batch([env.DB.prepare(`DELETE FROM care_patient_event_home WHERE event_id=?1 AND clinic_id=?2 AND patient_id=?3`).bind(eventId,patient.clinic_id,patientId),env.DB.prepare(`DELETE FROM care_patient_events WHERE id=?1 AND clinic_id=?2 AND patient_id=?3 AND source='home' AND created_by=?4`).bind(eventId,patient.clinic_id,patientId,s.home_user_id)]);await homeRecordAudit(env,patient,s.home_user_id,'home.record_deleted',eventId);return {ok:true,id:eventId}}

async function homeFollowupUser(env,s){const user=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT id,privacy_version FROM care_home_users WHERE id=?1 AND status='active'`).bind(s.home_user_id).first();if(!user)throw Object.assign(new Error('Carestep Home 계정을 찾지 못했습니다.'),{statusCode:401});if(user.privacy_version!==HOME_PRIVACY_VERSION)throw Object.assign(new Error('후속관리 안내와 보호자 응답 처리 안내에 먼저 동의해주세요.'),{statusCode:428});return user}
async function homeFollowupRow(env,s,id){await homeFollowupUser(env,s);const row=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT f.*,p.name_cipher patient_name_cipher,c.name clinic_name FROM care_home_followups f JOIN care_home_links l ON l.clinic_id=f.clinic_id AND l.guardian_id=f.guardian_id AND l.home_user_id=?1 AND l.status='active' JOIN care_patients p ON p.id=f.patient_id AND p.clinic_id=f.clinic_id AND p.guardian_id=f.guardian_id JOIN clinics c ON c.id=f.clinic_id AND c.status='active' WHERE f.id=?2 AND f.status<>'canceled' LIMIT 1`).bind(s.home_user_id,id).first();if(!row)throw Object.assign(new Error('확인할 후속관리 안내를 찾지 못했습니다.'),{statusCode:404});return row}
async function homeFollowupList(env,s,url){await homeFollowupUser(env,s);const limit=Math.min(300,Math.max(1,Number(url.searchParams.get('limit'))||100)),result=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT f.*,p.name_cipher patient_name_cipher,c.name clinic_name,COALESCE((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM care_home_consult_messages m WHERE m.clinic_id=f.clinic_id AND m.followup_id=f.id),0) consult_message_count,COALESCE((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM care_home_consult_messages m WHERE m.clinic_id=f.clinic_id AND m.followup_id=f.id AND m.sender_type='clinic'),0) consult_clinic_reply_count,COALESCE((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM care_home_consult_messages m WHERE m.clinic_id=f.clinic_id AND m.followup_id=f.id AND m.sender_type='clinic' AND m.read_at=''),0) consult_unread_count,COALESCE((SELECT MAX(m.created_at) FROM care_home_consult_messages m WHERE m.clinic_id=f.clinic_id AND m.followup_id=f.id),'') consult_last_at FROM care_home_followups f JOIN care_home_links l ON l.clinic_id=f.clinic_id AND l.guardian_id=f.guardian_id AND l.home_user_id=?1 AND l.status='active' JOIN care_patients p ON p.id=f.patient_id AND p.clinic_id=f.clinic_id AND p.guardian_id=f.guardian_id JOIN clinics c ON c.id=f.clinic_id AND c.status='active' WHERE f.status<>'canceled' ORDER BY CASE WHEN f.response_code='concern' AND COALESCE((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM care_home_consult_messages m WHERE m.clinic_id=f.clinic_id AND m.followup_id=f.id AND m.sender_type='clinic' AND m.read_at=''),0)>0 THEN 0 WHEN f.status='completed' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,f.due_date ASC,f.stage_day ASC LIMIT ?2`).bind(s.home_user_id,limit).all(),items=[];for(const row of (result.results||[]))items.push(await homeFollowupDto(env,row));const today=new Date(Date.now()+9*60*60*1000).toISOString().slice(0,10);return {ok:true,items,summary:{today:items.filter(x=>x.dueDate===today&&x.status!=='completed').length,overdue:items.filter(x=>x.dueDate<today&&x.status!=='completed').length,upcoming:items.filter(x=>x.dueDate>today&&x.status!=='completed').length,completed:items.filter(x=>x.status==='completed').length,consultation:items.filter(x=>x.responseCode==='concern'&&((x.consultation?.clinicReplyCount||0)===0||(x.consultation?.unreadCount||0)>0)).length},today}}
async function homeFollowupRead(env,s,id){const row=await homeFollowupRow(env,s,id),now=new Date().toISOString();if(row.status==='scheduled')await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_followups SET status='seen',read_at=?1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2 AND status='scheduled'`).bind(now,id).run();return {ok:true,item:await homeFollowupDto(env,{...row,status:row.status==='scheduled'?'seen':row.status,read_at:row.read_at||now,updated_at:now})}}
async function homeFollowupRespond(request,env,s,id){
  const row=await homeFollowupRow(env,s,id),today=new Date(Date.now()+9*60*60*1000).toISOString().slice(0,10);if(row.due_date>today)throw Object.assign(new Error('예정일이 된 후 확인 응답을 남길 수 있습니다.'),{statusCode:409});let b={};try{b=await request.json()}catch{}
  const responseCode=patientEnum(b.responseCode,HOME_FOLLOWUP_RESPONSES,''),note=clean(b.note,500);if(!responseCode)throw Object.assign(new Error('현재 상태를 선택해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});const now=new Date().toISOString(),actionStatus=responseCode==='concern'?'open':'none',noteCipher=await patientDataEncrypt(env,note);
  await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_followups SET status='completed',response_code=?1,response_note_cipher=?2,read_at=CASE WHEN read_at='' THEN ?3 ELSE read_at END,completed_at=?3,completed_by=?4,clinic_action_status=?5,updated_at=?3 WHERE id=?6`).bind(responseCode,noteCipher,now,s.home_user_id,actionStatus,id).run();
  if(responseCode==='concern')await ensureHomeConsultationSeed(env,{...row,response_code:responseCode,response_note_cipher:noteCipher,completed_by:s.home_user_id,completed_at:now});
  await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_patient_audit(id,clinic_id,user_id,action,target_type,target_id,created_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,'home.followup_responded','home_followup',?4,?5)`).bind('pcma_'+crypto.randomUUID(),row.clinic_id,s.home_user_id,id,now).run();return {ok:true,id,status:'completed',responseCode,clinicActionStatus:actionStatus,completedAt:now};
}

/* ================= v10.1-G · HOME CONSULTATION REPLY ================= */
async function ensureHomeConsultationSeed(env,row){
  if(row?.response_code!=='concern')return;
  const sourceRef=`followup-response:${row.id}`,found=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT id FROM care_home_consult_messages WHERE clinic_id=?1 AND source_ref=?2 LIMIT 1`).bind(row.clinic_id,sourceRef).first();if(found)return;
  const messageCipher=row.response_note_cipher||await patientDataEncrypt(env,'병원 상담이 필요합니다.'),nameCipher=await patientDataEncrypt(env,'보호자'),createdAt=row.completed_at||row.updated_at||new Date().toISOString();
  await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO care_home_consult_messages(id,clinic_id,guardian_id,patient_id,followup_id,sender_type,sender_id,sender_name_cipher,message_cipher,source_ref,read_at,created_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,'guardian',?6,?7,?8,?9,'',?10)`).bind('chcm_'+crypto.randomUUID(),row.clinic_id,row.guardian_id,row.patient_id,row.id,row.completed_by||row.guardian_id,nameCipher,messageCipher,sourceRef,createdAt).run();
}
async function homeConsultationMessageDto(env,row){return {id:row.id,senderType:row.sender_type,senderName:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,row.sender_name_cipher)|| (row.sender_type==='clinic'?'병원':'보호자'),message:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,row.message_cipher),readAt:row.read_at||'',createdAt:row.created_at}}
async function homeConsultationThread(env,row){
  await ensureHomeConsultationSeed(env,row);const result=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT * FROM care_home_consult_messages WHERE clinic_id=?1 AND followup_id=?2 ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 200`).bind(row.clinic_id,row.id).all(),items=[];for(const message of (result.results||[]))items.push(await homeConsultationMessageDto(env,message));return items;
}
async function homeConsultationGet(env,s,id){const row=await homeFollowupRow(env,s,id);if(row.response_code!=='concern')throw Object.assign(new Error('병원 상담을 요청한 후속관리만 대화를 확인할 수 있습니다.'),{statusCode:409});return {ok:true,followupId:id,patientId:row.patient_id,clinicName:row.clinic_name||'',items:await homeConsultationThread(env,row)}}
async function homeConsultationRead(env,s,id){const row=await homeFollowupRow(env,s,id),now=new Date().toISOString();await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_consult_messages SET read_at=?1 WHERE clinic_id=?2 AND followup_id=?3 AND sender_type='clinic' AND read_at=''`).bind(now,row.clinic_id,id).run();return {ok:true,followupId:id,readAt:now}}
async function homeConsultationSend(request,env,s,id){
  const row=await homeFollowupRow(env,s,id);if(row.response_code!=='concern')throw Object.assign(new Error('먼저 현재 상태에서 병원 상담 필요를 선택해주세요.'),{statusCode:409});let b={};try{b=await request.json()}catch{}const message=clean(b.message,1000);if(!message)throw Object.assign(new Error('병원에 보낼 메시지를 입력해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});
  const last=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT created_at FROM care_home_consult_messages WHERE clinic_id=?1 AND followup_id=?2 AND sender_type='guardian' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`).bind(row.clinic_id,id).first();if(last&&Date.now()-Date.parse(last.created_at)<15000)throw Object.assign(new Error('메시지를 연속으로 보냈습니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해주세요.'),{statusCode:429});
  const now=new Date().toISOString(),messageId='chcm_'+crypto.randomUUID();await env.DB.batch([env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_home_consult_messages(id,clinic_id,guardian_id,patient_id,followup_id,sender_type,sender_id,sender_name_cipher,message_cipher,source_ref,read_at,created_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,'guardian',?6,?7,?8,'','',?9)`).bind(messageId,row.clinic_id,row.guardian_id,row.patient_id,id,s.home_user_id,await patientDataEncrypt(env,'보호자'),await patientDataEncrypt(env,message),now),env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_followups SET clinic_action_status='open',clinic_assignee='',clinic_snoozed_until='',clinic_acknowledged_at='',clinic_acknowledged_by='',clinic_resolved_at='',clinic_resolved_by='',updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2 AND clinic_id=?3`).bind(now,id,row.clinic_id),env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_patient_audit(id,clinic_id,user_id,action,target_type,target_id,created_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,'home.consult_message_sent','home_followup',?4,?5)`).bind('pcma_'+crypto.randomUUID(),row.clinic_id,s.home_user_id,id,now)]);return {ok:true,messageId,followupId:id,items:await homeConsultationThread(env,{...row})};
}
async function clinicConsultationRow(env,s,id){
  requireRole(s,['owner','admin','vet','staff']);await assertFeature(env,s,'followupAutomation');const row=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT f.*,p.name_cipher patient_name_cipher,g.name_cipher guardian_name_cipher,c.name clinic_name FROM care_home_followups f JOIN care_patients p ON p.id=f.patient_id AND p.clinic_id=f.clinic_id JOIN care_guardians g ON g.id=f.guardian_id AND g.clinic_id=f.clinic_id JOIN clinics c ON c.id=f.clinic_id WHERE f.id=?1 AND f.clinic_id=?2 AND f.status<>'canceled' LIMIT 1`).bind(id,s.clinic_id).first();if(!row)throw Object.assign(new Error('상담 후속관리 항목을 찾지 못했습니다.'),{statusCode:404});if(row.response_code!=='concern')throw Object.assign(new Error('보호자가 병원 상담을 요청한 항목이 아닙니다.'),{statusCode:409});return row;
}
async function saasConsultationGet(env,s,id){const row=await clinicConsultationRow(env,s,id);return {ok:true,followupId:id,patientId:row.patient_id,patientName:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,row.patient_name_cipher),guardianName:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,row.guardian_name_cipher),title:await patientDataDecryptSafe(env,row.title_cipher),items:await homeConsultationThread(env,row)}}
async function saasConsultationRead(env,s,id){const row=await clinicConsultationRow(env,s,id),now=new Date().toISOString();await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_consult_messages SET read_at=?1 WHERE clinic_id=?2 AND followup_id=?3 AND sender_type='guardian' AND read_at=''`).bind(now,row.clinic_id,id).run();return {ok:true,followupId:id,readAt:now}}
function homeConsultationPushPayload(followupId=''){return {type:'consultation_reply',followupId:clean(followupId,150),title:'Carestep Home 상담 답변',body:'병원에서 상담 답변을 보냈습니다. 앱에서 확인해주세요.',url:`./home.html?followup=${encodeURIComponent(followupId)}`,tag:`carestep-consult-${clean(followupId,80)}`}}
async function deliverHomeConsultationPush(env,row,messageId){
  if(!pushConfig(env).enabled)return {sent:0,failed:0,skipped:'vapid_not_configured'};const result=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT l.home_user_id,s.* FROM care_home_links l JOIN care_home_users u ON u.id=l.home_user_id AND u.status='active' AND u.privacy_version=?1 JOIN care_home_push_subscriptions s ON s.home_user_id=l.home_user_id AND s.status='active' WHERE l.clinic_id=?2 AND l.guardian_id=?3 AND l.status='active' ORDER BY s.updated_at DESC LIMIT 10`).bind(HOME_PRIVACY_VERSION,row.clinic_id,row.guardian_id).all();let sent=0,failed=0;
  for(const subscription of (result.results||[])){const now=new Date().toISOString(),deliveryId='chcpd_'+crypto.randomUUID();await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_home_consult_push_deliveries(id,message_id,subscription_id,home_user_id,status,response_status,last_error,attempted_at,sent_at,created_at,updated_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,'pending',0,'',?5,'',?5,?5) ON CONFLICT(message_id,subscription_id) DO UPDATE SET status='pending',response_status=0,last_error='',attempted_at=excluded.attempted_at,updated_at=excluded.updated_at`).bind(deliveryId,messageId,subscription.id,subscription.home_user_id,now).run();try{const push=await pushSend(env,subscription,homeConsultationPushPayload(row.id)),done=new Date().toISOString();if(push.ok){sent++;await env.DB.batch([env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_consult_push_deliveries SET status='sent',response_status=?1,sent_at=?2,updated_at=?2 WHERE message_id=?3 AND subscription_id=?4`).bind(push.status,done,messageId,subscription.id),env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET failure_count=0,last_success_at=?1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,subscription.id)]);}else{failed++;await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_consult_push_deliveries SET status='failed',response_status=?1,last_error=?2,updated_at=?3 WHERE message_id=?4 AND subscription_id=?5`).bind(push.status,`push_http_${push.status}`,done,messageId,subscription.id).run();if(push.gone)await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET status='disabled',failure_count=failure_count+1,disabled_at=?1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,subscription.id).run();else await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET failure_count=failure_count+1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,subscription.id).run();}}catch(e){failed++;const done=new Date().toISOString();await env.DB.batch([env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_consult_push_deliveries SET status='failed',last_error=?1,updated_at=?2 WHERE message_id=?3 AND subscription_id=?4`).bind(clean(e?.message||'push_send_failed',160),done,messageId,subscription.id),env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET failure_count=failure_count+1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,subscription.id)]);}}
  return {sent,failed};
}
async function saasConsultationReply(request,env,s,id){
  const row=await clinicConsultationRow(env,s,id);await ensureHomeConsultationSeed(env,row);let b={};try{b=await request.json()}catch{}const message=clean(b.message,1000);if(!message)throw Object.assign(new Error('보호자에게 보낼 답변을 입력해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});const resolve=b.resolve!==false,now=new Date().toISOString(),messageId='chcm_'+crypto.randomUUID(),assignee=clean(s.display_name||s.email||s.user_id,120),fromStatus=row.clinic_action_status||'open';
  const claim=await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_followups SET clinic_action_status='in_progress',clinic_assignee=?1,clinic_snoozed_until='',clinic_acknowledged_at=CASE WHEN clinic_acknowledged_at='' THEN ?2 ELSE clinic_acknowledged_at END,clinic_acknowledged_by=?3,updated_at=?2 WHERE id=?4 AND clinic_id=?5 AND (clinic_action_status<>'in_progress' OR clinic_acknowledged_by IN('',?3))`).bind(assignee,now,s.user_id,id,row.clinic_id).run();if(Number(claim?.meta?.changes||0)!==1)throw Object.assign(new Error('다른 직원이 이 상담을 처리 중입니다. 업무함을 새로고침해주세요.'),{statusCode:409});
  await env.DB.batch([env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_home_consult_messages(id,clinic_id,guardian_id,patient_id,followup_id,sender_type,sender_id,sender_name_cipher,message_cipher,source_ref,read_at,created_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,'clinic',?6,?7,?8,'','',?9)`).bind(messageId,row.clinic_id,row.guardian_id,row.patient_id,id,s.user_id,await patientDataEncrypt(env,assignee||'병원'),await patientDataEncrypt(env,message),now),env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_consult_messages SET read_at=?1 WHERE clinic_id=?2 AND followup_id=?3 AND sender_type='guardian' AND read_at=''`).bind(now,row.clinic_id,id),env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_followups SET clinic_action_status=?1,clinic_assignee=?2,clinic_snoozed_until='',clinic_acknowledged_at=CASE WHEN clinic_acknowledged_at='' THEN ?3 ELSE clinic_acknowledged_at END,clinic_acknowledged_by=CASE WHEN clinic_acknowledged_by='' THEN ?4 ELSE clinic_acknowledged_by END,clinic_resolved_at=?5,clinic_resolved_by=?6,updated_at=?3 WHERE id=?7 AND clinic_id=?8`).bind(resolve?'resolved':'in_progress',assignee,now,s.user_id,resolve?now:'',resolve?s.user_id:'',id,row.clinic_id),env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_operation_audit(id,clinic_id,source_type,source_id,action,from_status,to_status,assignee,actor_user_id,detail_json,created_at) VALUES(?1,?2,'home',?3,'consultation.reply',?4,?5,?6,?7,?8,?9)`).bind('coa_'+crypto.randomUUID(),row.clinic_id,id,fromStatus,resolve?'resolved':'in_progress',assignee,s.user_id,JSON.stringify({messageId,resolve}),now)]);
  await patientCrmAudit(env,s,'home.consultation_replied','home_followup',id);const push=await deliverHomeConsultationPush(env,row,messageId);return {ok:true,messageId,followupId:id,resolved:resolve,push,items:await homeConsultationThread(env,row)};
}

/* ================= v10.0-D.1 · CARESTEP HOME WEB PUSH ================= */
function pushBytes(...parts){const arrays=parts.map(x=>x instanceof Uint8Array?x:new Uint8Array(x)),size=arrays.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.length,0),out=new Uint8Array(size);let offset=0;for(const part of arrays){out.set(part,offset);offset+=part.length}return out}
function pushConfig(env){
  const publicKey=clean(env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY,200),privateKey=clean(env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY,200),subject=clean(env.VAPID_SUBJECT,300);let publicBytes,privateBytes;
  try{publicBytes=fromB64u(publicKey);privateBytes=fromB64u(privateKey)}catch{}
  const enabled=!!(publicBytes?.length===65&&publicBytes[0]===4&&privateBytes?.length===32&&/^(mailto:|https:\/\/)/i.test(subject));
  return {enabled,publicKey:enabled?publicKey:'',publicBytes,privateBytes,subject};
}
async function pushHkdf(ikm,salt,info,length){const key=await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw',ikm,'HKDF',false,['deriveBits']);return new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.deriveBits({name:'HKDF',hash:'SHA-256',salt,info},key,length*8))}
function pushJoseSignature(input){const sig=input instanceof Uint8Array?input:new Uint8Array(input);if(sig.length===64)return sig;if(sig[0]!==0x30)throw new Error('VAPID 서명 형식을 확인할 수 없습니다.');let offset=2;if(sig[1]&0x80)offset=2+(sig[1]&0x7f);if(sig[offset++]!==0x02)throw new Error('VAPID R 서명 형식 오류');const rlen=sig[offset++],r=sig.slice(offset,offset+rlen);offset+=rlen;if(sig[offset++]!==0x02)throw new Error('VAPID S 서명 형식 오류');const slen=sig[offset++],s=sig.slice(offset,offset+slen),out=new Uint8Array(64);out.set(r.slice(Math.max(0,r.length-32)),32-Math.min(32,r.length));out.set(s.slice(Math.max(0,s.length-32)),64-Math.min(32,s.length));return out}
async function pushVapidAuthorization(endpoint,config){
  const x=b64u(config.publicBytes.slice(1,33)),y=b64u(config.publicBytes.slice(33,65)),d=b64u(config.privateBytes),key=await crypto.subtle.importKey('jwk',{kty:'EC',crv:'P-256',x,y,d,ext:true},{name:'ECDSA',namedCurve:'P-256'},false,['sign']);
  const enc=new TextEncoder(),head=b64u(enc.encode(JSON.stringify({typ:'JWT',alg:'ES256'}))),body=b64u(enc.encode(JSON.stringify({aud:new URL(endpoint).origin,exp:Math.floor(Date.now()/1000)+43200,sub:config.subject}))),unsigned=`${head}.${body}`,signature=pushJoseSignature(await crypto.subtle.sign({name:'ECDSA',hash:'SHA-256'},key,enc.encode(unsigned)));
  return `vapid t=${unsigned}.${b64u(signature)}, k=${b64u(config.publicBytes)}`;
}
async function pushEncryptPayload(payload,p256dh,auth){
  const clientPublic=fromB64u(p256dh),authSecret=fromB64u(auth);if(clientPublic.length!==65||clientPublic[0]!==4||authSecret.length<16)throw new Error('푸시 구독 키 형식이 올바르지 않습니다.');
  const clientKey=await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw',clientPublic,{name:'ECDH',namedCurve:'P-256'},false,[]),serverKeys=await crypto.subtle.generateKey({name:'ECDH',namedCurve:'P-256'},true,['deriveBits']),serverPublic=new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.exportKey('raw',serverKeys.publicKey)),shared=new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.deriveBits({name:'ECDH',public:clientKey},serverKeys.privateKey,256)),zero=new Uint8Array([0]),enc=new TextEncoder();
  const ikm=await pushHkdf(shared,authSecret,pushBytes(enc.encode('WebPush: info'),zero,clientPublic,serverPublic),32),salt=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)),cek=await pushHkdf(ikm,salt,pushBytes(enc.encode('Content-Encoding: aes128gcm'),zero),16),nonce=await pushHkdf(ikm,salt,pushBytes(enc.encode('Content-Encoding: nonce'),zero),12),plain=pushBytes(enc.encode(JSON.stringify(payload)),new Uint8Array([2]));
  if(plain.length>3800)throw new Error('푸시 알림 내용이 너무 깁니다.');
  const cipher=new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.encrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv:nonce},await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw',cek,{name:'AES-GCM'},false,['encrypt']),plain)),rs=new Uint8Array(4);new DataView(rs.buffer).setUint32(0,4096);
  return pushBytes(salt,rs,new Uint8Array([serverPublic.length]),serverPublic,cipher);
}
async function pushSend(env,subscription,payload){
  const config=pushConfig(env);if(!config.enabled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Worker의 VAPID 설정이 완료되지 않았습니다.'),{statusCode:503});const endpoint=await patientDataDecrypt(env,subscription.endpoint_cipher),p256dh=await patientDataDecrypt(env,subscription.p256dh_cipher),auth=await patientDataDecrypt(env,subscription.auth_cipher),body=await pushEncryptPayload(payload,p256dh,auth),authorization=await pushVapidAuthorization(endpoint,config),response=await fetch(endpoint,{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:authorization,'Content-Encoding':'aes128gcm','Content-Type':'application/octet-stream',TTL:'86400',Urgency:'normal'},body});
  return {ok:response.ok,status:response.status,gone:response.status===404||response.status===410};
}
function pushSubscriptionInput(body={}){const endpoint=clean(body?.endpoint,2000),keys=body?.keys||{},p256dh=clean(keys.p256dh,300),auth=clean(keys.auth,200),userAgent=clean(body?.userAgent,200);let url,pub,secret;try{url=new URL(endpoint);pub=fromB64u(p256dh);secret=fromB64u(auth)}catch{}if(!url||url.protocol!=='https:'||pub?.length!==65||pub[0]!==4||!secret||secret.length<16)throw Object.assign(new Error('브라우저 푸시 구독 정보를 확인해주세요.'),{statusCode:400});return {endpoint,p256dh,auth,userAgent}}
async function homePushStatus(env,s){await homeFollowupUser(env,s);const config=pushConfig(env),row=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM care_home_push_subscriptions WHERE home_user_id=?1 AND status='active'`).bind(s.home_user_id).first();return {ok:true,enabled:config.enabled,publicKey:config.publicKey,activeCount:Number(row?.n)||0}}
async function homePushSubscribe(request,env,s){await homeFollowupUser(env,s);if(!pushConfig(env).enabled)throw Object.assign(new Error('앱 푸시 서버 설정이 아직 완료되지 않았습니다.'),{statusCode:503});let body={};try{body=await request.json()}catch{}const p=pushSubscriptionInput(body),hash=await shaHex(`carestep.home.push.v1:${p.endpoint}`),old=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT id,created_at FROM care_home_push_subscriptions WHERE endpoint_hash=?1`).bind(hash).first(),id=old?.id||'chps_'+crypto.randomUUID(),now=new Date().toISOString();await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_home_push_subscriptions(id,home_user_id,endpoint_hash,endpoint_cipher,p256dh_cipher,auth_cipher,status,user_agent,failure_count,last_success_at,disabled_at,created_at,updated_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6,'active',?7,0,'','',?8,?8) ON CONFLICT(endpoint_hash) DO UPDATE SET home_user_id=excluded.home_user_id,endpoint_cipher=excluded.endpoint_cipher,p256dh_cipher=excluded.p256dh_cipher,auth_cipher=excluded.auth_cipher,status='active',user_agent=excluded.user_agent,failure_count=0,disabled_at='',updated_at=excluded.updated_at`).bind(id,s.home_user_id,hash,await patientDataEncrypt(env,p.endpoint),await patientDataEncrypt(env,p.p256dh),await patientDataEncrypt(env,p.auth),p.userAgent,now).run();return {ok:true,id,active:true}}
async function homePushUnsubscribe(request,env,s){await homeFollowupUser(env,s);let body={};try{body=await request.json()}catch{}const endpoint=clean(body.endpoint,2000);if(!endpoint)throw Object.assign(new Error('해제할 푸시 구독 정보가 없습니다.'),{statusCode:400});const hash=await shaHex(`carestep.home.push.v1:${endpoint}`),now=new Date().toISOString();await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET status='disabled',disabled_at=?1,updated_at=?1 WHERE endpoint_hash=?2 AND home_user_id=?3`).bind(now,hash,s.home_user_id).run();return {ok:true,active:false}}
function pushSafePayload(followupId=''){return {type:'followup',followupId:clean(followupId,150),title:'Carestep Home 관리 알림',body:'오늘 확인할 건강관리 안내가 있습니다.',url:`./home.html${followupId?`?followup=${encodeURIComponent(followupId)}`:''}`,tag:followupId?`carestep-followup-${clean(followupId,80)}`:'carestep-home-test'}}
async function homePushTest(env,s){await homeFollowupUser(env,s);const rows=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT * FROM care_home_push_subscriptions WHERE home_user_id=?1 AND status='active' ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 5`).bind(s.home_user_id).all();if(!(rows.results||[]).length)throw Object.assign(new Error('먼저 이 기기에서 앱 알림을 켜주세요.'),{statusCode:409});let sent=0,failed=0;for(const row of rows.results||[]){try{const result=await pushSend(env,row,{...pushSafePayload(''),body:'Carestep Home 시험 알림이 정상적으로 연결되었습니다.'});if(result.ok){sent++;await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET failure_count=0,last_success_at=?1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(new Date().toISOString(),row.id).run()}else{failed++;if(result.gone)await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET status='disabled',disabled_at=?1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(new Date().toISOString(),row.id).run()}}catch{failed++}}if(!sent)throw Object.assign(new Error('시험 알림을 보내지 못했습니다. 브라우저 알림 권한과 VAPID 설정을 확인해주세요.'),{statusCode:502});return {ok:true,sent,failed}}
async function pushDeliverFollowup(env,row){
  const now=new Date().toISOString(),deliveryId=row.delivery_id||'chpd_'+crypto.randomUUID();await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_home_push_deliveries(id,followup_id,subscription_id,home_user_id,status,attempt_count,response_status,last_error,first_attempt_at,last_attempt_at,sent_at,created_at,updated_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,'pending',1,0,'',?5,?5,'',?5,?5) ON CONFLICT(followup_id,subscription_id) DO UPDATE SET status='pending',attempt_count=care_home_push_deliveries.attempt_count+1,response_status=0,last_error='',first_attempt_at=CASE WHEN care_home_push_deliveries.first_attempt_at='' THEN excluded.first_attempt_at ELSE care_home_push_deliveries.first_attempt_at END,last_attempt_at=excluded.last_attempt_at,updated_at=excluded.updated_at`).bind(deliveryId,row.followup_id,row.subscription_id,row.home_user_id,now).run();
  try{const result=await pushSend(env,row,pushSafePayload(row.followup_id)),done=new Date().toISOString();if(result.ok){await env.DB.batch([env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_deliveries SET status='sent',response_status=?1,last_error='',sent_at=?2,updated_at=?2 WHERE followup_id=?3 AND subscription_id=?4`).bind(result.status,done,row.followup_id,row.subscription_id),env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET failure_count=0,last_success_at=?1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,row.subscription_id)]);return true}const err=`push_http_${result.status}`;await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_deliveries SET status='failed',response_status=?1,last_error=?2,updated_at=?3 WHERE followup_id=?4 AND subscription_id=?5`).bind(result.status,err,done,row.followup_id,row.subscription_id).run();if(result.gone)await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET status='disabled',failure_count=failure_count+1,disabled_at=?1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,row.subscription_id).run();else await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET failure_count=failure_count+1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,row.subscription_id).run();return false}catch(e){const done=new Date().toISOString();await env.DB.batch([env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_deliveries SET status='failed',last_error=?1,updated_at=?2 WHERE followup_id=?3 AND subscription_id=?4`).bind(clean(e?.message||'push_send_failed',160),done,row.followup_id,row.subscription_id),env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET failure_count=failure_count+1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,row.subscription_id)]);return false}
}
async function runHomePushCycle(env){await ensureSaasDb(env);if(!pushConfig(env).enabled)return {ok:false,skipped:'vapid_not_configured'};const today=new Date(Date.now()+9*60*60*1000).toISOString().slice(0,10),oldest=new Date(Date.now()+9*60*60*1000-7*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10),result=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT f.id followup_id,l.home_user_id,s.id subscription_id,s.endpoint_cipher,s.p256dh_cipher,s.auth_cipher,d.id delivery_id FROM care_home_followups f JOIN care_home_links l ON l.clinic_id=f.clinic_id AND l.guardian_id=f.guardian_id AND l.status='active' JOIN care_home_users u ON u.id=l.home_user_id AND u.status='active' AND u.privacy_version=?1 JOIN care_home_push_subscriptions s ON s.home_user_id=l.home_user_id AND s.status='active' LEFT JOIN care_home_push_deliveries d ON d.followup_id=f.id AND d.subscription_id=s.id WHERE f.status IN('scheduled','seen') AND f.due_date BETWEEN ?2 AND ?3 AND COALESCE(d.status,'')<>'sent' ORDER BY f.due_date,f.stage_day LIMIT 300`).bind(HOME_PRIVACY_VERSION,oldest,today).all(),rows=result.results||[];let sent=0,failed=0;for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i+=10){const batch=await Promise.all(rows.slice(i,i+10).map(row=>pushDeliverFollowup(env,row)));sent+=batch.filter(Boolean).length;failed+=batch.filter(x=>!x).length}return {ok:true,checked:rows.length,sent,failed,today}}

/* ================= v10.0-D.2 · PREVENTIVE CARE WEB PUSH ================= */
function preventiveReminderMeta(row,today){
  const due=patientEventDate(row?.next_due_date||'',false);if(!due)return null;
  const days=Math.round((Date.parse(`${due}T00:00:00Z`)-Date.parse(`${today}T00:00:00Z`))/86400000),type=row?.event_type;
  if(type==='vaccination'&&days>=1&&days<=7)return {key:'before',scheduledFor:today,daysUntil:days};
  if(type==='heartworm'&&days>=1&&days<=3)return {key:'before',scheduledFor:today,daysUntil:days};
  if((type==='vaccination'||type==='heartworm')&&days<=0&&days>=-7)return {key:'due',scheduledFor:today,daysUntil:days};
  return null;
}
function preventiveVaccineCode(sourceRef=''){return String(sourceRef||'').match(/^vaccination-group:[^:]+:([^:]+)$/)?.[1]||''}
async function preventiveSuperseded(env,row,today){
  const base=`SELECT id FROM care_patient_events WHERE clinic_id=?1 AND patient_id=?2 AND event_type=?3 AND status<>'canceled' AND event_date>?4 AND event_date<=?5`;
  if(row.event_type==='vaccination'){
    const code=preventiveVaccineCode(row.source_ref),newer=code
      ?await env.DB.prepare(base+` AND source_ref LIKE ?6 LIMIT 1`).bind(row.clinic_id,row.patient_id,'vaccination',row.event_date,today,`%:${code}`).first()
      :await env.DB.prepare(base+` LIMIT 1`).bind(row.clinic_id,row.patient_id,'vaccination',row.event_date,today).first();
    return !!newer;
  }
  return !!(await env.DB.prepare(base+` LIMIT 1`).bind(row.clinic_id,row.patient_id,'heartworm',row.event_date,today).first());
}
function pushSafePreventivePayload(patientId='',eventId='',phase='before'){
  const query=new URLSearchParams();if(patientId)query.set('patient',clean(patientId,160));if(eventId)query.set('event',clean(eventId,160));
  return {type:'preventive',patientId:clean(patientId,160),eventId:clean(eventId,160),title:'Carestep Home 예방관리 알림',body:phase==='due'?'오늘 확인할 예방관리 일정이 있습니다. 앱에서 확인해주세요.':'예방관리 예정일이 다가오고 있습니다. 앱에서 확인해주세요.',url:`./home.html${query.toString()?`?${query.toString()}`:''}`,tag:`carestep-preventive-${clean(eventId,80)}-${phase}`};
}
function preventiveGroupKey(row){return `${row.event_type}:${row.clinic_id}:${row.patient_id}:${row.next_due_date}`}
async function pushDeliverPreventive(env,row,meta,groupKey,delivery){
  const now=new Date().toISOString(),deliveryId=delivery?.id||'chprd_'+crypto.randomUUID();
  await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO care_home_preventive_push_deliveries(id,event_id,patient_id,subscription_id,home_user_id,reminder_group_key,reminder_key,scheduled_for,status,attempt_count,response_status,last_error,first_attempt_at,last_attempt_at,sent_at,created_at,updated_at) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6,?7,?8,'pending',1,0,'',?9,?9,'',?9,?9) ON CONFLICT(reminder_group_key,subscription_id,reminder_key) DO UPDATE SET event_id=excluded.event_id,patient_id=excluded.patient_id,scheduled_for=excluded.scheduled_for,status='pending',attempt_count=care_home_preventive_push_deliveries.attempt_count+1,response_status=0,last_error='',first_attempt_at=CASE WHEN care_home_preventive_push_deliveries.first_attempt_at='' THEN excluded.first_attempt_at ELSE care_home_preventive_push_deliveries.first_attempt_at END,last_attempt_at=excluded.last_attempt_at,updated_at=excluded.updated_at`).bind(deliveryId,row.event_id,row.patient_id,row.subscription_id,row.home_user_id,groupKey,meta.key,meta.scheduledFor,now).run();
  try{
    const result=await pushSend(env,row,pushSafePreventivePayload(row.patient_id,row.event_id,meta.key)),done=new Date().toISOString();
    if(result.ok){await env.DB.batch([env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_preventive_push_deliveries SET status='sent',response_status=?1,last_error='',sent_at=?2,updated_at=?2 WHERE reminder_group_key=?3 AND subscription_id=?4 AND reminder_key=?5`).bind(result.status,done,groupKey,row.subscription_id,meta.key),env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET failure_count=0,last_success_at=?1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,row.subscription_id)]);return 'sent'}
    const err=`push_http_${result.status}`;await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_preventive_push_deliveries SET status='failed',response_status=?1,last_error=?2,updated_at=?3 WHERE reminder_group_key=?4 AND subscription_id=?5 AND reminder_key=?6`).bind(result.status,err,done,groupKey,row.subscription_id,meta.key).run();
    if(result.gone)await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET status='disabled',failure_count=failure_count+1,disabled_at=?1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,row.subscription_id).run();else await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET failure_count=failure_count+1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,row.subscription_id).run();return 'failed';
  }catch(e){const done=new Date().toISOString();await env.DB.batch([env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_preventive_push_deliveries SET status='failed',last_error=?1,updated_at=?2 WHERE reminder_group_key=?3 AND subscription_id=?4 AND reminder_key=?5`).bind(clean(e?.message||'push_send_failed',160),done,groupKey,row.subscription_id,meta.key),env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_push_subscriptions SET failure_count=failure_count+1,updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2`).bind(done,row.subscription_id)]);return 'failed'}
}
async function preparePreventivePushCandidate(env,row,today){
  const meta=preventiveReminderMeta(row,today);if(!meta)return 'outside_window';
  if(await preventiveSuperseded(env,row,today))return 'superseded';
  return {row,meta,groupKey:preventiveGroupKey(row)};
}
async function processPreventivePushGroup(env,item){
  const {row,meta,groupKey}=item,delivery=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT id,status FROM care_home_preventive_push_deliveries WHERE reminder_group_key=?1 AND subscription_id=?2 AND reminder_key=?3 LIMIT 1`).bind(groupKey,row.subscription_id,meta.key).first();
  if(delivery?.status==='sent')return 'already_sent';
  return pushDeliverPreventive(env,row,meta,groupKey,delivery);
}
async function reconcilePreventivePushDeliveries(env){
  const rows=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT d.id,d.reminder_group_key,e.id event_id,e.event_type,e.clinic_id,e.patient_id,e.next_due_date,e.status event_status FROM care_home_preventive_push_deliveries d LEFT JOIN care_patient_events e ON e.id=d.event_id WHERE d.status IN('pending','failed') LIMIT 500`).all(),cancel=[];
  for(const row of (rows.results||[])){const expected=row.event_id?`${row.event_type}:${row.clinic_id}:${row.patient_id}:${row.next_due_date||''}`:'';if(!row.event_id||row.event_status==='canceled'||!row.next_due_date||row.reminder_group_key!==expected)cancel.push(row.id)}
  if(cancel.length){const now=new Date().toISOString();for(let i=0;i<cancel.length;i+=50)await env.DB.batch(cancel.slice(i,i+50).map(id=>env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE care_home_preventive_push_deliveries SET status='canceled',last_error='schedule_changed_or_canceled',updated_at=?1 WHERE id=?2 AND status IN('pending','failed')`).bind(now,id)))}
  return cancel.length;
}
async function runHomePreventivePushCycle(env){
  await ensureSaasDb(env);if(!pushConfig(env).enabled)return {ok:false,skipped:'vapid_not_configured'};
  const reconciled=await reconcilePreventivePushDeliveries(env);
  const today=new Date(Date.now()+9*60*60*1000).toISOString().slice(0,10),oldest=new Date(Date.now()+9*60*60*1000-7*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10),latest=new Date(Date.now()+9*60*60*1000+7*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10);
  const result=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT e.id event_id,e.clinic_id,e.patient_id,e.event_type,e.event_date,e.next_due_date,e.source_ref,l.home_user_id,s.id subscription_id,s.endpoint_cipher,s.p256dh_cipher,s.auth_cipher FROM care_patient_events e JOIN care_patients p ON p.id=e.patient_id AND p.clinic_id=e.clinic_id JOIN clinics c ON c.id=e.clinic_id AND c.status='active' JOIN care_home_links l ON l.clinic_id=e.clinic_id AND l.guardian_id=p.guardian_id AND l.status='active' JOIN care_home_users u ON u.id=l.home_user_id AND u.status='active' AND u.privacy_version=?1 JOIN care_home_push_subscriptions s ON s.home_user_id=l.home_user_id AND s.status='active' LEFT JOIN care_patient_event_home h ON h.event_id=e.id AND h.clinic_id=e.clinic_id WHERE e.event_type IN('vaccination','heartworm') AND e.status<>'canceled' AND e.source IN('clinic','system') AND e.next_due_date BETWEEN ?2 AND ?3 AND COALESCE(h.home_visible,1)=1 ORDER BY e.next_due_date,e.event_date LIMIT 300`).bind(HOME_PRIVACY_VERSION,oldest,latest).all(),rows=result.results||[],outcomes=[],prepared=[];
  for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i+=10)prepared.push(...await Promise.all(rows.slice(i,i+10).map(row=>preparePreventivePushCandidate(env,row,today))));
  outcomes.push(...prepared.filter(x=>typeof x==='string'));const groups=new Map();for(const item of prepared){if(typeof item==='string')continue;const key=`${item.row.subscription_id}:${item.groupKey}:${item.meta.key}`;if(!groups.has(key))groups.set(key,item)}const jobs=[...groups.values()];
  for(let i=0;i<jobs.length;i+=10)outcomes.push(...await Promise.all(jobs.slice(i,i+10).map(item=>processPreventivePushGroup(env,item))));
  const count=key=>outcomes.filter(x=>x===key).length;return {ok:true,checked:rows.length,sent:count('sent'),failed:count('failed'),superseded:count('superseded'),alreadySent:count('already_sent'),outsideWindow:count('outside_window'),reconciled,today};
}

export default {
  async fetch(request, env){
    const publicUrl=new URL(request.url);
    if(publicUrl.pathname.startsWith('/survey/')){try{return await publicGuardianSurvey(request,env,publicUrl);}catch(e){return new Response(guardianSurveyPageShell('응답 오류',`<h1 class="title">응답 화면을 열 수 없습니다</h1><div class="sub">잠시 후 다시 시도하거나 병원으로 연락해주세요.</div>`),{status:e.statusCode||500,headers:{'Content-Type':'text/html; charset=utf-8','Cache-Control':'no-store'}});}}
    if(request.method==='GET'&&publicUrl.pathname==='/saas/messaging/oauth/callback')return solapiOauthCallback(request,env,publicUrl);
    const origin=allowedOrigin(request,env);
    if(origin===null) return json({ok:false,error:'허용되지 않은 웹사이트 Origin입니다.'},403,'');

    if(request.method==='OPTIONS'){
      if(!origin) return new Response(null,{status:204});
      return new Response(null,{status:204,headers:{
        'Access-Control-Allow-Origin':origin,'Vary':'Origin','Access-Control-Allow-Headers':'Authorization, Content-Type, X-CARESTEP-ADMIN-KEY, X-CARESTEP-HQ-KEY','Access-Control-Allow-Methods':'GET, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE, OPTIONS','Access-Control-Max-Age':'600'
      }});
    }

    const url=new URL(request.url);
    const model=String(env.OPENAI_MODEL||'gpt-5.6-terra');

    // v10.0-D.1 Carestep Home account, timeline, follow-up delivery, and Web Push routes.
    if(url.pathname.startsWith('/home/')){
      try{
        const hi=url.pathname.match(/^\/home\/invite\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='GET'&&hi)return json(await homeInvitePreview(env,decodeURIComponent(hi[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&hi)return json(await homeInviteAccept(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hi[1])),201,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/home/login')return json(await homeLogin(request,env),200,origin||'');
        const homeSession=await homeSessionOf(request,env,true);
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/home/me')return json({ok:true,...await homeMe(env,homeSession),session:{expiresAt:homeSession.expires_at}},200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/home/health-consent')return json(await homeHealthConsent(request,env,homeSession),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/home/followups')return json(await homeFollowupList(env,homeSession,url),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/home/push')return json(await homePushStatus(env,homeSession),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/home/push/subscriptions')return json(await homePushSubscribe(request,env,homeSession),201,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&url.pathname==='/home/push/subscriptions')return json(await homePushUnsubscribe(request,env,homeSession),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/home/push/test')return json(await homePushTest(env,homeSession),200,origin||'');
        const hfc=url.pathname.match(/^\/home\/followups\/([^/]+)\/consultation(?:\/(read|messages))?$/);
        if(request.method==='GET'&&hfc&&!hfc[2])return json(await homeConsultationGet(env,homeSession,decodeURIComponent(hfc[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&hfc&&hfc[2]==='read')return json(await homeConsultationRead(env,homeSession,decodeURIComponent(hfc[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&hfc&&hfc[2]==='messages')return json(await homeConsultationSend(request,env,homeSession,decodeURIComponent(hfc[1])),201,origin||'');
        const hfr=url.pathname.match(/^\/home\/followups\/([^/]+)\/(read|respond)$/);
        if(request.method==='POST'&&hfr&&hfr[2]==='read')return json(await homeFollowupRead(env,homeSession,decodeURIComponent(hfr[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&hfr&&hfr[2]==='respond')return json(await homeFollowupRespond(request,env,homeSession,decodeURIComponent(hfr[1])),200,origin||'');
        const hpt=url.pathname.match(/^\/home\/patients\/([^/]+)\/timeline$/);
        if(request.method==='GET'&&hpt)return json(await homePatientTimeline(env,homeSession,url,decodeURIComponent(hpt[1])),200,origin||'');
        const hpr=url.pathname.match(/^\/home\/patients\/([^/]+)\/records$/);
        if(request.method==='POST'&&hpr)return json(await homePatientRecordCreate(request,env,homeSession,decodeURIComponent(hpr[1])),201,origin||'');
        const hpre=url.pathname.match(/^\/home\/patients\/([^/]+)\/records\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='PUT'&&hpre)return json(await homePatientRecordUpdate(request,env,homeSession,decodeURIComponent(hpre[1]),decodeURIComponent(hpre[2])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&hpre)return json(await homePatientRecordDelete(env,homeSession,decodeURIComponent(hpre[1]),decodeURIComponent(hpre[2])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/home/logout'){await env.DB.prepare(`DELETE FROM care_home_sessions WHERE id=?1`).bind(homeSession.session_id).run();return json({ok:true},200,origin||'')}
        return json({ok:false,error:'Not found'},404,origin||'');
      }catch(e){return json({ok:false,error:clean(e.message||'Carestep Home API 오류',500)},e.statusCode||500,origin||'')}
    }


    // v10.1-A Partner API accepts only scoped, hashed partner keys.
    if(url.pathname.startsWith('/partner/v1/')){
      let ps=null,statusCode=200;
      try{
        ps=await partnerApiSession(request,env);
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/partner/v1/health')return json({ok:true,service:'CARESTEP Partner API',version:CARESTEP_VERSION,partner:{code:ps.partner_code,name:ps.partner_name},scopes:ps.scopeList},200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/partner/v1/clinics')return json(await partnerApiClinics(env,ps),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/partner/v1/subscriptions')return json(await partnerApiSubscriptions(env,ps),200,origin||'');
        statusCode=404;return json({ok:false,error:'Not found'},404,origin||'');
      }catch(e){statusCode=e.statusCode||500;return json({ok:false,error:clean(e.message||'Partner API 오류',500)},statusCode,origin||'');}
      finally{if(ps)await partnerApiAudit(env,ps,request,statusCode);}
    }

    // HQ Admin Console uses a dedicated HQ secret and never accepts clinic or partner tokens as HQ authority.
    if(url.pathname.startsWith('/hq/')){
      if(!hqAuthorized(request,env)) return json({ok:false,error:'CARESTEP HQ Key가 올바르지 않습니다.'},401,origin||'');
      try{
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/health') return json({ok:true,service:'CARESTEP HQ Admin',version:CARESTEP_VERSION,db:!!env.DB,patientCrm:true,partnerChannel:true},200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/overview') return json(await hqOverview(env),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/onboarding') return json(await hqOnboardingOverview(env),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/customer-success') return json(await hqCustomerSuccessOverview(env),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/integrations/calendar') return json(await hqGetCalendarIntegration(env),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='PUT'&&url.pathname==='/hq/integrations/calendar') return json(await hqPutCalendarIntegration(request,env),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/integrations/messaging') return json(await hqMessagingStatus(env),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/messaging/sender-onboarding') return json(await hqSenderOnboardingList(env,url),200,origin||'');
        const hso=url.pathname.match(/^\/hq\/messaging\/sender-onboarding\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&hso) return json(await hqReviewSenderOnboarding(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hso[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/clinics') return json(await hqClinics(env,url),200,origin||'');
        const hchannel=url.pathname.match(/^\/hq\/clinics\/([^/]+)\/channel$/);
        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&hchannel) return json(await hqSetClinicChannel(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hchannel[1])),200,origin||'');
        const hc=url.pathname.match(/^\/hq\/clinics\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='GET'&&hc) return json(await hqClinicDetail(env,decodeURIComponent(hc[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&hc) return json(await hqPatchClinic(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hc[1])),200,origin||'');
        const hca=url.pathname.match(/^\/hq\/clinics\/([^/]+)\/approval$/);
        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&hca) return json(await hqPatchApproval(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hca[1])),200,origin||'');
        const hcs=url.pathname.match(/^\/hq\/clinics\/([^/]+)\/subscription$/);
        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&hcs) return json(await hqPatchSubscription(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hcs[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/plans') return json(await hqPlans(env),200,origin||'');
        const hp=url.pathname.match(/^\/hq\/plans\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&hp) return json(await hqPatchPlan(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hp[1])),200,origin||'');

        const hv=url.pathname.match(/^\/hq\/voice\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&hv) return json(await hqPatchVoice(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hv[1])),200,origin||'');

        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&hco) return json(await hqPatchCategoryOverride(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hco[1])),200,origin||'');

        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/incidents') return json(await hqIncidents(env,url),200,origin||'');
        const hin=url.pathname.match(/^\/hq\/incidents\/([^/]+)$/);if(request.method==='PATCH'&&hin) return json(await hqPatchIncident(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hin[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/backups') return json(await hqBackups(env,url),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/hq/backups') return json(await hqCreateBackup(request,env),201,origin||'');
        const hbr=url.pathname.match(/^\/hq\/backups\/([^/]+)\/restore$/);if(request.method==='POST'&&hbr) return json(await hqRestoreBackup(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hbr[1])),200,origin||'');
