        const hdr=url.pathname.match(/^\/hq\/deletion-requests\/([^/]+)\/execute$/);if(request.method==='POST'&&hdr) return json(await hqExecuteDeletion(request,env,decodeURIComponent(hdr[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/hq/audit') return json(await hqAuditList(env),200,origin||'');
        return json({ok:false,error:'Not found'},404,origin||'');
      }catch(e){return json({ok:false,error:clean(e.message||'HQ API 오류',500),details:e.details||undefined},e.statusCode||500,origin||'');}
    }

    // v6.3.2 SaaS hardening routes use a per-user session token instead of CARESTEP_ACCESS_KEY.
    if(url.pathname.startsWith('/saas/')){
      let ses=null;
      try{
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/register') return json(await apiRegister(request,env),201,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/login') return json(await apiLogin(request,env),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/invite/accept') return json(await apiAcceptInvite(request,env),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/legal/docs') return json(legalPublicDocs(),200,origin||'');
        ses=await sessionOf(request,env,true);
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/me') return json({ok:true,me:await meOf(env,ses),session:{id:ses.session_id,createdAt:ses.session_created_at,lastSeenAt:ses.last_seen_at,expiresAt:ses.expires_at}},200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/entitlements') return json({ok:true,entitlements:await entitlementForClinic(env,ses.clinic_id,ses.plan||'PILOT')},200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/legal/status') return json(await saasLegalStatus(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/legal/accept') return json(await saasAcceptLegal(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/backups') return json(await saasBackups(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/backups') return json(await saasCreateBackup(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        const sbr=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/backups\/([^/]+)\/restore$/);if(request.method==='POST'&&sbr) return json(await saasRestoreBackup(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(sbr[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/clinic/deletion-request') return json(await saasDeletionStatus(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/clinic/deletion-request') return json(await saasRequestClinicDeletion(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/clinic/deletion-request/cancel') return json(await saasCancelClinicDeletion(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/account/withdraw') return json(await saasWithdrawAccount(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/integrations/calendar') return json(await saasCalendarIntegration(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/patient-directory') return json(await patientDirectory(env,ses,url),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/guardians') return json(await patientCreateGuardian(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        const ghi=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/guardians\/([^/]+)\/home-invite$/);
        if(request.method==='POST'&&ghi) return json(await clinicCreateHomeInvite(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(ghi[1])),201,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&ghi) return json(await clinicRevokeHomeInvite(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(ghi[1])),200,origin||'');
        const pg=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/guardians\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='GET'&&pg) return json(await patientGuardianDetail(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(pg[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='PUT'&&pg) return json(await patientUpdateGuardian(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(pg[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&pg) return json(await patientDeleteGuardian(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(pg[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/patients') return json(await patientCreatePatient(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        const ptl=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/patients\/([^/]+)\/timeline$/);
        if(request.method==='GET'&&ptl) return json(await patientTimelineList(env,ses,url,decodeURIComponent(ptl[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&ptl) return json(await patientTimelineCreate(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(ptl[1])),201,origin||'');
        const phf=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/patients\/([^/]+)\/home-followups$/);
        if(request.method==='GET'&&phf) return json(await clinicListHomeFollowups(env,ses,url,decodeURIComponent(phf[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&phf) return json(await clinicScheduleHomeFollowups(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(phf[1])),201,origin||'');
        const phfi=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/patients\/([^/]+)\/home-followups\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&phfi) return json(await clinicCancelHomeFollowup(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(phfi[1]),decodeURIComponent(phfi[2])),200,origin||'');
        const pte=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/patients\/([^/]+)\/timeline\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='PUT'&&pte) return json(await patientTimelineUpdate(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(pte[1]),decodeURIComponent(pte[2])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&pte) return json(await patientTimelineDelete(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(pte[1]),decodeURIComponent(pte[2])),200,origin||'');
        const pp=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/patients\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='PUT'&&pp) return json(await patientUpdatePatient(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(pp[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&pp) return json(await patientDeletePatient(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(pp[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/patient-journeys') return json(await saasListPatientJourneys(env,ses,url),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/patient-journeys') return json(await saasCreatePatientJourney(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        const pjc=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/patient-journeys\/([^/]+)\/clone$/);
        if(request.method==='POST'&&pjc) return json(await saasClonePatientJourney(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(pjc[1])),201,origin||'');
        const pj=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/patient-journeys\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='PUT'&&pj) return json(await saasUpdatePatientJourney(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(pj[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&pj) return json(await saasDeletePatientJourney(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(pj[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/surveys') return json(await saasListGuardianSurveys(env,ses,url),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/crm/overview') return json(await saasFollowupCrmOverview(env,ses,url),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/followup/schedule-board') return json(await saasFollowupScheduleBoard(env,ses,url),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/care-operations') return json(await saasCareOperations(env,ses,url),200,origin||'');
        const hfa=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/home-followups\/([^/]+)\/action$/);
        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&hfa) return json(await saasUpdateHomeFollowupAction(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(hfa[1])),200,origin||'');
        const hfc=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/home-followups\/([^/]+)\/consultation(?:\/(read|reply))?$/);
        if(request.method==='GET'&&hfc&&!hfc[2]) return json(await saasConsultationGet(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(hfc[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&hfc&&hfc[2]==='read') return json(await saasConsultationRead(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(hfc[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&hfc&&hfc[2]==='reply') return json(await saasConsultationReply(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(hfc[1])),201,origin||'');
        const pra=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/preventive-events\/([^/]+)\/recall$/);
        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&pra) return json(await saasUpdatePreventiveRecall(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(pra[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/crm/outcomes') return json(await saasSyncCrmOutcome(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        const crv=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/crm\/cases\/([^/]+)\/review$/);
        if(request.method==='POST'&&crv) return json(await saasReviewCrmCase(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(crv[1])),200,origin||'');
        const gsa=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/surveys\/([^/]+)\/ack$/);
        if(request.method==='POST'&&gsa) return json(await saasAcknowledgeGuardianSurvey(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(gsa[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/messaging/settings') return json(await saasMessagingSettings(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/messaging/oauth/start') return json(await solapiOauthStart(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&url.pathname==='/saas/messaging/oauth') return json(await saasSolapiDisconnect(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/messaging/senders') return json(await saasSolapiSenders(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/messaging/kakao/channels') return json(await saasKakaoChannels(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/messaging/kakao/templates') return json(await saasKakaoTemplates(env,ses,url),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/messaging/sender-onboarding') return json(await saasRequestSenderOnboarding(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='PUT'&&url.pathname==='/saas/messaging/settings') return json(await saasPutMessagingSettings(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/messaging/test/status') return json(await saasMessagingTestStatus(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/messaging/test') return json(await saasMessagingTest(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/followup/messages/schedule') return json(await saasScheduleFollowupMessages(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/followup/messages') return json(await saasListFollowupMessages(env,ses,url),200,origin||'');
        const fma=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/followup\/messages\/([^/]+)\/ack$/);
        if(request.method==='POST'&&fma) return json(await saasAcknowledgeMessageFailure(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(fma[1])),200,origin||'');
        const fmr=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/followup\/messages\/([^/]+)\/retry$/);
        if(request.method==='POST'&&fmr) return json(await saasRetryFollowupMessage(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(fmr[1])),201,origin||'');
        const fm=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/followup\/messages\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&fm) return json(await saasCancelFollowupMessage(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(fm[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/ai/draft'){if(!hqAuthorized(request,env))return json({ok:false,error:'AI Content Studio는 CARESTEP 운영자 전용입니다.'},403,origin||'');return json(await apiSaasAiDraft(request,env,ses,origin||'',model),200,origin||'');}
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/logout'){await env.DB.prepare(`DELETE FROM saas_sessions WHERE token_hash=?1`).bind(ses.tokenHash).run();return json({ok:true},200,origin||'');}
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/password/change') return json(await apiChangePassword(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/sessions') return json(await apiSessions(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        const sm=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/sessions\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&sm) return json(await apiRevokeSession(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(sm[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/switch-clinic') return json(await apiSwitchClinic(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/members') return json(await apiMembers(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/invites') return json(await apiInvite(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        const mm=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/members\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&mm) return json(await apiPatchMember(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(mm[1])),200,origin||'');
        const mr=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/members\/([^/]+)\/revoke-sessions$/);
        if(request.method==='POST'&&mr) return json(await apiRevokeMemberSessions(env,ses,decodeURIComponent(mr[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/clinic/state') return json(await apiGetState(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='PUT'&&url.pathname==='/saas/clinic/state') return json(await apiPutState(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/usage') return json(await apiUsage(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/usage/summary') return json(await apiUsageSummary(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/onboarding') return json(await saasOnboardingStatus(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/onboarding/mark') return json(await saasOnboardingMark(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/reports/monthly') return json(await saasMonthlyOperationsReport(env,ses,url),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/billing/summary') return json(await saasBillingSummary(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/payments/overview') return json(await billingOverview(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/payments/setup-intent') return json(await createBillingSetupIntent(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/payments/setup-complete') return json(await finalizeBillingSetup(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/payments/subscription/plan') return json(await scheduleSubscriptionPlan(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/payments/subscription/cancel') return json(await cancelSubscription(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/payments/retry') return json(await manualBillingRetry(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/client-error') return json(await apiClientError(request,env,ses),201,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/diagnostics') return json(await apiDiagnostics(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/content/registry') return json(await apiRegistry(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/content/category-overrides') return json(await categoryOverridesList(env),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/saas/content/published') return json(await apiPublishedContent(env,ses),200,origin||'');
        const sac=url.pathname.match(/^\/saas\/content\/admin\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='PATCH'&&sac) return json(await apiAdminPatchCentralContent(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(sac[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='DELETE'&&sac) return json(await apiAdminDeleteCentralContent(request,env,ses,decodeURIComponent(sac[1])),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/content/admin/migrate') return json(await apiMigrateCentralContent(request,env,ses),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/saas/content/admin/repair-shorts') return json(await apiRepairCentralDuplicateShorts(request,env,ses,model),200,origin||'');
        return json({ok:false,error:'Not found'},404,origin||'');
      }catch(e){await logSaasError(env,ses,'api:'+url.pathname,e);return json({ok:false,error:clean(e.message||'SaaS API 오류',500),details:e.details||undefined},e.statusCode||500,origin||'');}
    }

    // CUSTOMER VOICE: public submission from allowed CARESTEP origin. No AI access key required.
    if(request.method==='POST' && url.pathname==='/voice/request'){
      if(!origin) return json({ok:false,error:'브라우저 Origin이 필요한 요청입니다.'},403,'');
      try{return await handleVoicePublic(request,env,origin);}catch(e){return json({ok:false,error:clean(e.message||'Customer Voice DB 오류',500)},500,origin||'');}
    }

    // CUSTOMER VOICE admin endpoints use a separate admin secret.
    if(url.pathname.startsWith('/voice/admin/')){
      if(!adminAuthorized(request,env)) return json({ok:false,error:'CARESTEP Admin Key가 올바르지 않습니다.'},401,origin||'');
      try{
        if(request.method==='GET' && url.pathname==='/voice/admin/summary') return json(await handleVoiceSummary(env),200,origin||'');
        if(request.method==='GET' && url.pathname==='/voice/admin/requests') return json(await handleVoiceList(url,env),200,origin||'');
        const m=url.pathname.match(/^\/voice\/admin\/request\/([^/]+)$/);
        if(request.method==='PATCH' && m){const out=await handleVoicePatch(request,env,decodeURIComponent(m[1]));return json(out,out.ok?200:(out.statusCode||400),origin||'');}
        return json({ok:false,error:'Not found'},404,origin||'');
      }catch(e){return json({ok:false,error:clean(e.message||'Customer Voice DB 오류',500)},500,origin||'');}
    }

    // Existing AI routes continue to use CARESTEP_ACCESS_KEY.
    if(!authorized(request,env)) return json({ok:false,error:'CARESTEP Access Key가 올바르지 않습니다.'},401,origin||'');

    if(request.method==='GET' && (url.pathname==='/' || url.pathname==='/health')){
      return json({ok:true,service:'CARESTEP AI Gateway',version:CARESTEP_VERSION,model,customerVoice:!!env.DB,multiClinic:!!env.DB,contentCloud:!!env.DB,patientCrm:!!env.DB,partnerChannel:!!env.DB},200,origin||'');
    }

    if(request.method!=='POST' || url.pathname!=='/draft') return json({ok:false,error:'Not found'},404,origin||'');
    if(!env.OPENAI_API_KEY) return json({ok:false,error:'Worker에 OPENAI_API_KEY Secret이 설정되지 않았습니다.'},503,origin||'');

    const len=Number(request.headers.get('Content-Length')||0);
    if(len>30000) return json({ok:false,error:'요청 크기가 너무 큽니다.'},413,origin||'');

    let body;
    try{ body=await request.json(); }catch{ return json({ok:false,error:'JSON 요청 형식이 아닙니다.'},400,origin||''); }
    const payload=sanitizeBody(body);
    if(!payload.brief.condition) return json({ok:false,error:'질환/관리주제가 필요합니다.'},400,origin||'');

    const aiResponse=await fetch(OPENAI_URL,{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${env.OPENAI_API_KEY}`,'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({
        model,
        store:false,
        input:[
          {role:'system',content:systemPrompt()},
          {role:'user',content:userPrompt(payload)}
        ],
        max_output_tokens:10000,
        text:{format:{type:'json_schema',name:'carestep_veterinary_content_draft',strict:true,schema:OUTPUT_SCHEMA}}
      })
    });

    let data={};
    try{data=await aiResponse.json();}catch{}
    if(!aiResponse.ok){
      const msg=clean(data?.error?.message||`OpenAI API HTTP ${aiResponse.status}`,500);
      return json({ok:false,error:msg},502,origin||'');
    }
    if(data.status==='incomplete') return json({ok:false,error:'AI 응답이 출력 한도에서 중단되었습니다. 입력 메모를 줄이거나 다시 시도해주세요.'},502,origin||'');

    let parsed;
    try{
      const text=extractText(data);
      if(!text) throw new Error('AI 응답에서 JSON 텍스트를 찾지 못했습니다.');
      parsed=JSON.parse(text);
    }catch(e){ return json({ok:false,error:clean(e.message||'AI JSON 파싱 실패',500)},502,origin||''); }

    const draft=toCarestepDraft(parsed);
    return json({ok:true,draft,model:data.model||model,requestId:data.id||'',usage:data.usage?{inputTokens:data.usage.input_tokens||0,cachedInputTokens:data.usage.input_tokens_details?.cached_tokens||0,outputTokens:data.usage.output_tokens||0,reasoningOutputTokens:data.usage.output_tokens_details?.reasoning_tokens||0,totalTokens:data.usage.total_tokens||0}:null},200,origin||'');
  },
  async scheduled(event,env,ctx){
    const cron=String(event?.cron||'');
    if(cron==='*/5 * * * *'){
      ctx.waitUntil(runTrackedCron(env,'mms_fallback_5m',()=>runMmsFallbackCycle(env)).catch(e=>console.error('CARESTEP MMS fallback cron failed',e)));
      return;
    }
    ctx.waitUntil((async()=>{await ensureSaasDb(env);const tasks=[['subscription_billing_daily',()=>runSubscriptionBillingCycle(env)],['home_followup_push_daily',()=>runHomePushCycle(env)],['preventive_push_daily',()=>runHomePreventivePushCycle(env)],['mms_fallback_daily',()=>runMmsFallbackCycle(env)]];await Promise.allSettled(tasks.map(([name,fn])=>runTrackedCron(env,name,fn).catch(e=>{console.error(`CARESTEP ${name} cron failed`,e);throw e})))} )());
  }
};
